# Agents · T · part 5

1,003 entries, test-runner to theory-verifier. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **test-runner**: Use this agent to run an upstream repo's native test suite (pnpm/yarn/npm/pytest/cargo/sbt/composer/bundle), log to ~/.contribute-system/test-logs/. - **testing-specialist.guards.knowledge**: - A **method whose RETURN TYPE widens** (`Future&lt;void&gt;`→`Future&lt;bool&gt;`) — every existing call site discards it and the suite stays green under `return true`. - **testing-specialist.mock-harness.knowledge**: - **"Duplicate test" is measurable**: mutate the guarded expression; a duplicate deletes only a strict subset of the kill set THROUGH THE SAME SEAM. - **testing-specialist.parsing-tagging-menu.knowledge**: - **A green probe on a "nothing changes" test is usually GUARD-CHAIN SUBSUMPTION, not a live mutant** — an earlier early-return fires and the guard under test is never reached.
